1) * healed_page_size\n                    end_idx = start_idx + healed_page_size\n                    paginated_entries = filtered_entries[start_idx:end_idx]\n\n                    pagination_info = {\n                        \"page\": healed_page,\n                        \"page_size\": healed_page_size,\n                        \"total_entries\": len(filtered_entries),\n                        \"has_next\": end_idx < len(filtered_entries),\n                        \"has_prev\": healed_page > 1\n                    }\n                    validation_warnings.append(f\"Pagination corrected due to error: {str(pagination_error)}\")\n                else:\n", "line_end": 830, "line_start": 800}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tools/query_entries.py", "byte_size": 83016, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 11, "line_count": 2028, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tools/query_entries.py", "sha256": "962f81fda093ddd562017beff5a48dda61510e4bfcc066e1317cfccab492577a"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:35:12.975797Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:35:27 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 19526, "byte_start": 17778, "content": "        start = final_config.start\n        end = final_config.end\n        message = final_config.message\n        message_mode = final_config.message_mode\n        case_sensitive = final_config.case_sensitive\n        emoji = final_config.emoji\n        status = final_config.status\n        agents = final_config.agents\n        meta_filters = final_config.meta_filters\n        limit = final_config.limit\n        page = final_config.page\n        page_size = final_config.page_size\n        search_scope = final_config.search_scope\n        document_types = final_config.document_types\n        include_outdated = final_config.include_outdated\n        verify_code_references = final_config.verify_code_references\n        time_range = final_config.time_range\n        relevance_threshold = final_config.relevance_threshold\n        max_results = final_config.max_results\n\n        # Resolve project context using the already prepared context from the caller\n        project_context = context\n        resolved_project = None\n        if isinstance(project, dict):\n            resolved_project = project.get(\"name\") or project.get(\"project\") or project.get(\"id\")\n        if not resolved_project and getattr(context, \"project\", None):\n            resolved_project = (context.project or {}).get(\"name\")\n        if not resolved_project:\n            resolved_project = project_name or \"default\"\n\n        # Build search parameters dictionary\n        search_params = {\n            \"project\": resolved_project,\n            \"start\": start,\n            \"end\": end,\n            \"message\": message,\n            \"message_mode\": message_mode,\n            \"case_sensitive\": case_sensitive,\n            \"emoji\": emoji,\n            \"status\": status,\n            \"agents\": agents,\n", "line_end": 460, "line_start": 420}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tools/query_entries.py", "byte_size": 83016, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 11, "line_count": 2028, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tools/query_entries.py", "sha256": "962f81fda093ddd562017beff5a48dda61510e4bfcc066e1317cfccab492577a"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:35:27.322546Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:35:35 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 52951, "byte_start": 47104, "content": "    \"\"\"Search the project log with flexible filters and pagination.\n\n    Args:\n        project: Project name (uses active project if None)\n        start: Start timestamp filter\n        end: End timestamp filter\n        message: Message text filter\n        message_mode: How to match message (substring, regex, exact)\n        case_sensitive: Case sensitive message matching\n        emoji: Filter by emoji(s)\n        status: Filter by status(es) (mapped to emojis)\n        agents: Filter by agent name(s)\n        meta_filters: Filter by metadata key/value pairs\n        limit: Maximum results to return (legacy, for backward compatibility)\n        page: Page number for pagination (1-based)\n        page_size: Number of results per page\n        compact: Use compact response format with short field names\n        fields: Specific fields to include in response\n        include_metadata: Include metadata field in entries\n        # Phase 4 Enhanced Search Parameters\n        search_scope: Search scope - \"project\", \"global\", \"all_projects\", \"research\", \"bugs\", or \"all\"\n        document_types: Filter by document types - [\"progress\", \"research\", \"architecture\", \"bugs\", \"global\"]\n        include_outdated: Include outdated information with warnings\n        verify_code_references: Check if mentioned code still exists\n        time_range: Time range filter - \"last_30d\", \"last_7d\", \"today\", etc.\n        relevance_threshold: Minimum relevance score (0.0-1.0) for results\n        max_results: Override maximum results (deprecated, use limit/page_size instead)\n        config: Configuration object for dual parameter support. If provided, legacy parameters\n               take precedence when both are specified.\n        format: Output format - \"readable\" (human-friendly, default), \"structured\" (full JSON), \"compact\" (minimal)\n        # Phase 5 Priority/Category Filter Parameters\n        priority: Filter by priority levels (e.g., [\"critical\", \"high\"])\n        category: Filter by categories (e.g., [\"bug\", \"security\"])\n        min_confidence: Minimum confidence threshold (0.0-1.0)\n        priority_sort: If True, sort by priority (critical first) then by time\n\n    Returns:\n        Paginated response with entries and metadata\n    \"\"\"\n    # Phase 3 Task 3.5: Enhanced Function Decomposition\n    # This function now uses decomposed sub-functions with bulletproof error handling\n\n    # Back-compat: accept singular `agent=` and merge into `agents=`\n    if agent:\n        if agents is None:\n            agents = []\n        if isinstance(agent, str):\n            agents = [*agents, agent]\n        elif isinstance(agent, list):\n            agents = [*agents, *[a for a in agent if isinstance(a, str)]]\n\n    try:\n        state_snapshot = await server_module.state_manager.record_tool(\"query_entries\")\n    except Exception:\n        state_snapshot = {}\n\n    try:\n        # === PHASE 3 ENHANCED PARAMETER VALIDATION AND PREPARATION ===\n        # Replace monolithic parameter handling with bulletproof validation and healing\n        final_config, validation_info = _validate_search_parameters(\n            project=project,\n            start=start,\n            end=end,\n            message=message,\n            message_mode=message_mode,\n            case_sensitive=case_sensitive,\n            emoji=emoji,\n            status=status,\n            agents=agents,\n            meta_filters=meta_filters,\n            limit=limit,\n            page=page,\n            page_size=page_size,\n            compact=compact,\n            fields=fields,\n            priority=priority,\n            category=category,\n            min_confidence=min_confidence,\n            priority_sort=priority_sort,\n            include_metadata=include_metadata,\n            search_scope=search_scope,\n            document_types=document_types,\n            include_outdated=include_outdated,\n            verify_code_references=verify_code_references,\n            time_range=time_range,\n            relevance_threshold=relevance_threshold,\n            max_results=max_results,\n            config=config\n        )\n\n        # === CONTEXT RESOLUTION WITH ENHANCED ERROR HANDLING ===\n        try:\n            context = await resolve_logging_context(\n                tool_name=\"query_entries\",\n                server_module=server_module,\n                agent_id=None,\n                require_project=False,  # query_entries can work without active project\n                state_snapshot=state_snapshot,\n            )\n        except Exception as context_error:\n            # Apply Phase 2 ExceptionHealer for context resolution errors\n            healed_context = _EXCEPTION_HEALER.heal_parameter_validation_error(\n                context_error, {\"tool_name\": \"query_entries\"}\n            )\n\n            if healed_context and healed_context.get(\"success\"):\n                # Create minimal fallback context\n                context = type('obj', (object,), {\n                    'project': None,\n                    'recent_projects': [],\n                    'reminders': []\n                })()\n            else:\n                # Continue with empty context\n                context = type('obj', (object,), {\n                    'project': None,\n                    'recent_projects': [],\n                    'reminders': []\n                })()\n\n        project = context.project or {}\n\n        # === ENHANCED SEARCH QUERY BUILDING ===\n        search_query = _build_search_query(final_config, context, project)\n\n        if not search_query.get(\"query_built\", False):\n            # If query building failed, try to continue with emergency query\n            if search_query.get(\"emergency_fallback\"):\n                # Execute emergency search\n                search_result = await _execute_search_with_fallbacks(search_query, final_config)\n                search_result[\"parameter_healing\"] = True\n", "line_end": 1160, "line_start": 1030}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tools/query_entries.py", "byte_size": 83016, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 11, "line_count": 2028, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tools/query_entries.py", "sha256": "962f81fda093ddd562017beff5a48dda61510e4bfcc066e1317cfccab492577a"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:35:35.889685Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

Args:\\n        project: Project name (uses active project if None)\\n        start: Start timestamp filter\\n        end: End timestamp filter\\n        message: Message text filter\\n        message_mode: How to match message (substring, regex, exact)\\n        case_sensitive: Case sensitive message matching\\n        emoji: Filter by emoji(s)\\n        status: Filter by status(es) (mapped to emojis)\\n        agents: Filter by agent name(s)\\n        meta_filters: Filter by metadata key/value pairs\\n        limit: Maximum results to return (legacy, for backward compatibility)\\n        page: Page number for pagination (1-based)\\n        page_size: Number of results per page\\n        compact: Use compact response format with short field names\\n        fields: Specific fields to include in response\\n        include_metadata: Include metadata field in entries\\n        # Phase 4 Enhanced Search Parameters\\n        search_scope: Search scope - \\\"project\\\", \\\"global\\\", \\\"all_projects\\\", \\\"research\\\", \\\"bugs\\\", or \\\"all\\\"\\n        document_types: Filter by document types - [\\\"progress\\\", \\\"research\\\", \\\"architecture\\\", \\\"bugs\\\", \\\"global\\\"]\\n        include_outdated: Include outdated information with warnings\\n        verify_code_references: Check if mentioned code still exists\\n        time_range: Time range filter - \\\"last_30d\\\", \\\"last_7d\\\", \\\"today\\\", etc.\\n        relevance_threshold: Minimum relevance score (0.0-1.0) for results\\n        max_results: Override maximum results (deprecated, use limit/page_size instead)\\n        config: Configuration object for dual parameter support. If provided, legacy parameters\\n               take precedence when both are specified.\\n        format: Output format - \\\"readable\\\" (human-friendly, default), \\\"structured\\\" (full JSON), \\\"compact\\\" (minimal)\\n        # Phase 5 Priority/Category Filter Parameters\\n        priority: Filter by priority levels (e.g., [\\\"critical\\\", \\\"high\\\"])\\n        category: Filter by categories (e.g., [\\\"bug\\\", \\\"security\\\"])\\n        min_confidence: Minimum confidence threshold (0.0-1.0)\\n        priority_sort: If True, sort by priority (critical first) then by time\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        Paginated response with entries and metadata\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Phase 3 Task 3.5: Enhanced Function Decomposition\\n    # This function now uses decomposed sub-functions with bulletproof error handling\\n\\n    # Back-compat: accept singular `agent=` and merge into `agents=`\\n    if agent:\\n        if agents is None:\\n            agents = []\\n        if isinstance(agent, str):\\n            agents = [*agents, agent]\\n        elif isinstance(agent, list):\\n            agents = [*agents, *[a for a in agent if isinstance(a, str)]]\\n\\n    try:\\n [ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:48:38 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 7685, "byte_start": 5973, "content": "            def derive_session_identity(exec_context, arguments: dict) -> tuple[str, dict]:\n                \"\"\"Derive stable session identity from execution context.\n\n                Returns (identity_hash, identity_parts dict)\n                \"\"\"\n                # 1. Canonicalize repo_root\n                repo_root = os.path.realpath(exec_context.repo_root)\n\n                # 2. Get mode and scope_key\n                mode = exec_context.mode  # \"project\" or \"sentinel\"\n                if mode == \"sentinel\":\n                    scope_key = exec_context.sentinel_day  # e.g., \"2026-01-03\"\n                else:\n                    scope_key = exec_context.execution_id  # UUID\n\n                # 3. Get agent_key (prefer stable ID, fallback to display_name)\n                agent_key = None\n                if exec_context.agent_identity:\n                    agent_key = (\n                        getattr(exec_context.agent_identity, 'id', None) or\n                        getattr(exec_context.agent_identity, 'instance_id', None) or\n                        exec_context.agent_identity.display_name\n                    )\n                if not agent_key:\n                    agent_key = arguments.get(\"agent\") or \"default\"\n\n                # 4. Construct identity string\n                identity = f\"{repo_root}:{mode}:{scope_key}:{agent_key}\"\n\n                # 5. Hash it (full SHA-256, no truncation)\n                identity_hash = hashlib.sha256(identity.encode()).hexdigest()\n\n                return identity_hash, {\n                    \"repo_root\": repo_root,\n                    \"mode\": mode,\n                    \"scope_key\": scope_key,\n                    \"agent_key\": agent_key,\n                }\n\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 162}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/server.py", "byte_size": 26996, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 4, "line_count": 619, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "server.py", "sha256": "b5ea3b09b035866838266679c960fcec0dfe4ed1e244e55d768ffe002a7b5a92"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:48:38.446059Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

"content": "\"\"\"Shared logging utilities for Scribe MCP tools.\n\nThese helpers consolidate repeated normalization, project resolution, and\nresponse-building logic that previously lived in multiple tool modules.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, List, MutableMapping, Optional, Sequence, Tuple\nfrom collections.abc import Mapping\n\nfrom scribe_mcp import reminders\n\nMETA_KEY_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"^[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]+$\")\n\n\n@dataclass(slots=True)\nclass LoggingContext:\n    \"\"\"Resolved context information required by most logging tools.\"\"\"\n\n    tool_name: str\n    project: Optional[Dict[str, Any]]\n    recent_projects: List[str]\n    state_snapshot: Dict[str, Any]\n    reminders: List[Dict[str, Any]]\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None\n\n\nclass ProjectResolutionError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a project is required but cannot be resolved.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, message: str, recent_projects: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> None:\n        super().__init__(message)\n        self.recent_projects = list(recent_projects or [])\n\n\nasync def resolve_logging_context(\n    *,\n    tool_name: str,\n    server_module,\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    explicit_project: Optional[str] = None,\n    require_project: bool = True,\n    state_snapshot: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    reminder_variables: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n    operation_status: Optional[str] = None,\n", "line_end": 50, "line_start": 1}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, 76347, "content": "    agent: str,\n    meta_payload: Dict[str, Any],\n    log_cache: Dict[str, Tuple[Path, Dict[str, Any]]],\n) -> Tuple[Optional[Path], List[str]]:\n    \"\"\"Best-effort secondary write used to populate auxiliary logs (bugs/security/progress).\"\"\"\n    log_path, log_definition = _resolve_log_target(project, log_type, log_cache)\n    meta_copy = dict(meta_payload)\n    meta_copy[\"log_type\"] = log_type\n    missing = _missing_required_meta(log_definition, meta_copy)\n    if missing:\n        return None, missing\n\n    line = _compose_line(\n        emoji=emoji,\n        message=message,\n        timestamp=timestamp,\n        agent=agent,\n        project_name=project.get(\"name\", \"unknown\"),\n        meta_pairs=tuple(meta_copy.items()),\n    )\n    await append_line(\n        log_path,\n        line,\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n        context={\"component\": \"logs\", \"project_name\": project.get(\"name\")},\n    )\n    return log_path, []\n\n\ndef _make_missing_meta_reminder(\n    *,\n    target_log_type: str,\n    missing_keys: List[str],\n) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n    if target_log_type == \"bugs\":\n        example = \"meta={severity:high, component:auth, status:open}\"\n    elif target_log_type == \"security\":\n        example = \"meta={severity:high, area:sandbox, impact:path-escape}\"\n    else:\n        example = \"meta={...}\"\n\n    missing = \", \".join(missing_keys)\n    return {\n        \"level\": \"info\",\n        \"score\": 300,\n        \"emoji\": \"\ud83e\udde0\",\n        \"category\": \"teaching\",\n        \"message\": f\"To also write this entry to `{target_log_type}` log, include required meta keys: {missing} (example: {example}).\",\n        \"tone\": \"neutral\",\n [ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:56:27 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 10224, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "# \ud83d\udd2c Research: Tool Logging Architecture \u2014 scribe_tool_output_refinement\n**Author:** ResearchAgent\n**Version:** v1.0\n**Status:** Complete\n**Last Updated:** 2026-01-04 05:51:00 UTC\n\n> This document analyzes the current tool_logs implementation and designs a session-aware architecture with SQL metrics that eliminates the recursive append_entry pattern. It provides the foundation for implementing direct JSONL writes with database-backed analytics.\n\n---\n## Executive Summary\n<!-- ID: executive_summary -->\n**Primary Objective:** Design a session-aware tool logging system with SQL metrics that bypasses the recursive `append_entry` pattern currently causing massive JSON nesting in `finalize_tool_response()`.\n\n**Key Takeaways:**\n- **Current Implementation is Broken:** `finalize_tool_response()` calls `append_entry(log_type=\"tool_logs\")` which triggers another `finalize_tool_response()`, creating infinite recursion/nesting.\n- **Robust Infrastructure Exists:** Session tracking (`scribe_sessions` table), atomic file writing (`utils/files.py::append_line` with WAL), and log rotation are production-ready.\n- **SQL Schema Needed:** No `tool_calls` table exists; need new schema to store metrics (tool_name, duration, status, session_id, project_name).\n- **Direct Writing Solution:** Can write directly to JSONL using existing `append_line()` infrastructure, completely bypassing `append_entry`.\n- **No Reminders Required:** Tool logs are audit trails, not user-facing work logs\u2014reminder system must be explicitly disabled.\n\n---\n## Research Scope\n<!-- ID: research_scope -->\n**Research Lead:** ResearchAgent\n\n**Investigation Window:** 2026-01-04 (single-day focused research)\n\n**Focus Areas:**\n- [x] Current `tool_logs` implementation analysis\n- [x] Session tracking infrastructure (SQL tables, session derivation)\n- [x] Existing direct file writing patterns\n- [x] Log rotation and JSONL handling\n- [x] SQL schema design requirements\n\n**Dependencies & Constraints:**\n- Must preserve existing session tracking architecture (no breaking changes)\n- Must use Write-Ahead Log (WAL) pattern for crash safety\n- Must NOT trigger reminder system for tool log writes\n- Tool logs should remain JSONL format for backward compatibility\n- SQL metrics should support session-based analytics\n\n---\n## Findings\n<!-- ID: findings -->\n\n### Finding 1: Recursive append_entry Pattern is Root Cause\n- **Summary:** `utils/response.py:2164-2183` calls `append_entry(log_type=\"tool_logs\")` from within `finalize_tool_response()`, but `append_entry` itself calls `finalize_tool_response()`, creating recursion.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - File: `utils/response.py`, lines 2164-2183\n  - Guard exists (`if tool_name != \"append_entry\"`) but only prevents one level\u2014all other tools still recurse\n  - Uses `format=\"structured\"` to avoid formatting recursion, but fundamental design flaw remains\n- **Confidence:** **High (1.0)**\n\n### Finding 2: Session Infrastructure is Production-Ready\n- **Summary:** `scribe_sessions` table provides stable session tracking with `session_id` (SHA-256 hash), `transport_session_id`, `agent_id`, `repo_root`, and `mode` (sentinel project).\n- **Evidence:**\n  - File: `storage/sqlite.py`, lines 746-768\n  - File: `server.py`, lines 162-199 (`derive_session_identity()`)\n  - Session derivation: `SHA-256(repo_root:mode:scope_key:agent_key)`\n  - Sentinel mode uses day, project mode uses execution_id UUID\n- **Confidence:** **High (1.0)**\n\n### Finding 3: No Existing tool_calls Table\n- **Summary:** Grep search confirms no `tool_calls` or `tool_metrics` table exists in `storage/sqlite.py`. Tool metrics currently only exist in per-project JSONL files.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - File: `storage/sqlite.py` (grep: `CREATE TABLE.*tool` \u2192 no matches)\n  - TOOL_LOG.jsonl files exist per-project but no SQL backing\n- **Confidence:** **High (1.0)**\n\n### Finding 4: Atomic JSONL Writing Infrastructure Exists\n- **Summary:** `utils/files.py::append_line()` provides bulletproof atomic append with Write-Ahead Log (WAL) crash safety, already used by all log writes.\n- **Evidence:**\n  - File: `utils/files.py`, lines 516-543\n  - Function signature: `append_line(path, line, use_wal=True, repo_root, context)`\n  - Uses `_write_line_with_wal()` for atomic writes with rollback on failure\n  - Already used by `tools/append_entry.py` for all log types\n- **Confidence:** **High (1.0)**\n\n### Finding 5: Log Configuration Treats tool_logs as Standard Log Type\n- **Summary:** `config/log_config.json` defines `tool_logs` with JSONL format, rotation threshold of 1000 entries, and required metadata (`tool`, `format_requested`).\n- **Evidence:**\n  - File: `config/log_config.json`, lines 24-30\n  - Config routes tool_logs through standard `append_entry` infrastructure (causing recursion)\n  - Path template: `{docs_dir}/TOOL_LOG.jsonl`\n- **Confidence:** **High (1.0)**\n\n### Additional Notes\n- Log rotation infrastructure (`tools/rotate_log.py`) handles JSONL files correctly\n- Multiple TOOL_LOG.jsonl files exist across projects, confirming per-project isolation works\n- `.journal` and `.lock` files indicate WAL mechanism is actively used\n\n---\n## Technical Analysis\n<!-- ID: technical_analysis -->\n\n**Code Patterns Identified:**\n\n1. **Anti-Pattern: Recursive Tool Logging**\n   - `finalize_tool_response()` \u2192 `append_entry(log_type=\"tool_logs\")` \u2192 `finalize_tool_response()` \u2192 ...\n   - Guard only prevents `append_entry` from logging itself, not other tools\n   - Creates massive JSON nesting in responses\n\n2. **Pattern: Session Derivation**\n   - Central session identity derivation in `server.py::derive_session_identity()`\n   - SHA-256 hash ensures stability across tool calls within same session\n   - Transport session ID bridges initial connection to stable session\n\n3. **Pattern: WAL-Based Atomic Writes**\n   - `utils/files.py::append_line()` uses Write-Ahead Log pattern\n   - `.journal` file written first, then main file updated, then journal removed\n   - Crash-safe: incomplete writes can be rolled back or recovered\n\n4. **Pattern: Log Type Routing**\n   - `config/log_config.json` defines log types with paths, formats, metadata requirements\n   - All logs flow through `append_entry` \u2192 `_compose_line()` \u2192 `append_line()`\n   - Tool logs follow same pattern (causing recursion issue)\n\n**System Interactions:**\n\n- **Session Layer:** `server.py` derives session \u2192 stores in `scribe_sessions` table \u2192 links to `session_projects`\n- **Storage Layer:** `storage/sqlite.py` provides async DB operations, session management\n- **File Layer:** `utils/files.py` provides atomic file operations with WAL safety\n- **Tool Layer:** All tools call `finalize_tool_response()` which attempts to log the call\n\n**Risk Assessment:**\n\n- [x] **HIGH RISK:** Current recursion pattern creates token bloat, performance issues, and potential stack overflow\n- [x] **MEDIUM RISK:** Direct file writing bypasses reminder system\u2014must explicitly disable reminders for tool logs\n- [x] **LOW RISK:** Adding SQL table requires migration but doesn't break existing functionality\n- [x] **LOW RISK:** JSONL files may grow large over time\u2014rotation threshold of 1000 entries is reasonable\n\n---\n## Recommendations\n<!-- ID: recommendations -->\n\n### Immediate Next Steps\n\n1. **Create `tool_calls` SQL Table**\n   ```sql\n   CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_calls (\n       id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n       session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n       tool_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n       timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,\n       duration_ms REAL,\n       status TEXT CHECK (status IN ('success', 'error', 'partial')),\n       format_requested TEXT,\n       project_name TEXT,\n       agent_id TEXT,\n       error_message TEXT,\n       FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES scribe_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n   );\n   CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_session ON tool_calls(session_id);\n   CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_tool ON tool_calls(tool_name);\n   CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_timestamp ON tool_calls(timestamp);\n   ```\n\n2. **Implement Direct JSONL Writer**\n   - Create `utils/tool_logger.py` module with:\n     - `log_tool_call(session_id, tool_name, data, project_name, format_requested)` function\n     - Direct call to `append_line(TOOL_LOG.jsonl, json.dumps(entry))`\n     - Parallel SQL insert to `tool_calls` table\n     - **NO** calls to `append_entry` or `finalize_tool_response`\n     - **NO** reminder generation (explicitly disabled)\n\n3. **Update finalize_tool_response()**\n   - Replace lines 2164-2183 in `utils/response.py`\n   - Call new `log_tool_call()` function directly\n   - Remove recursive `append_entry` call\n   - Add guard to prevent ANY recursion into logging\n\n4. **Add Session Context to Tool Calls**\n   - Extract `session_id` from execution context in `server.py`\n   - Pass to `finalize_tool_response()` as parameter\n   - Include in both JSONL and SQL writes\n\n5. **Test Session-Based Queries**\n   - Implement query functions:\n     - `get_session_tool_calls(session_id)` \u2192 all tools in session\n     - `get_tool_metrics(tool_name, time_range)` \u2192 performance analytics\n     - `get_project_tool_usage(project_name)` \u2192 project activity stats\n\n### Long-Term Opportunities\n\n- **Analytics Dashboard:** SQL metrics enable real-time tool usage analytics, performance monitoring, error tracking\n- **Session Replay:** Complete audit trail allows replaying entire agent sessions for debugging\n- **Cost Tracking:** Tool call counts and durations enable API cost estimation and optimization\n- **Anomaly Detection:** Identify unusual tool usage patterns, performance degradation, error clusters\n- **Migration Path:** Existing JSONL files can be backfilled into SQL for historical analysis\n\n---\n## Appendix\n<!-- ID: appendix -->\n\n**File References:**\n- `/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/response.py:2164-2183` (current broken implementation)\n- `/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py:746-768` (scribe_sessions table)\n- `/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/server.py:162-199` (session derivation)\n- `/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/files.py:516-543` (append_line WAL implementation)\n- `/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/config/log_config.json:24-30` (tool_logs config)\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "estimated_chunk_count": 2, "line_count": 218, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/research/RESEARCH_TOOL_LOGGING_ARCH_20260104_0550.md", "sha256": "98cf559359a4fcbff41084eff0afca63cd25ca18491e9c0817bdb1a96810d639"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:56:27.568234Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:56:39 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 10994, "byte_start": 10224, "content": "\n**Key Design Decisions:**\n1. **JSONL + SQL Dual Write:** Maintain JSONL for backward compatibility, add SQL for analytics\n2. **Session-First Design:** All tool calls linked to session_id for traceability\n3. **No Reminders:** Tool logs are audit trails, not work logs\u2014reminder system disabled\n4. **Direct Writing:** Bypass append_entry completely to eliminate recursion\n5. **WAL Safety:** Reuse existing atomic write infrastructure for crash safety\n\n**Implementation Estimate:**\n- SQL schema: 1 hour\n- Direct writer module: 2-3 hours\n- finalize_tool_response update: 1 hour\n- Testing and validation: 2-3 hours\n- **Total: ~8 hours (1 development day)**\n\n---\n**Research Complete:** 2026-01-04 05:51:00 UTC\n**Next Phase:** Architecture design and implementation planning\n", "line_end": 218, "line_start": 201}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/research/RESEARCH_TOOL_LOGGING_ARCH_20260104_0550.md", "byte_size": 10994, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 2, "line_count": 218, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "docs/dev_plans/scribe_tool_output_refinement/research/RESEARCH_TOOL_LOGGING_ARCH_20260104_0550.md", "sha256": "98cf559359a4fcbff41084eff0afca63cd25ca18491e9c0817bdb1a96810d639"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:56:39.078898Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:56:46 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 90057, "byte_start": 89100, "content": "        # STEP 1: Log to tool_logs (audit trail) - SKIP for append_entry to avoid recursion\n        # append_entry calls finalize_tool_response, which would call append_entry again\n        if tool_name != \"append_entry\":\n            try:\n                from tools.append_entry import append_entry\n\n                await append_entry(\n                    message=f\"Tool call: {tool_name}\",\n                    log_type=\"tool_logs\",\n                    meta={\n                        \"tool\": tool_name,\n                        \"format_requested\": format,\n                        \"response_data\": data,\n                        \"timestamp\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + \"Z\"\n                    },\n                    format=\"structured\"  # Use structured to avoid nested formatting\n                )\n            except Exception as e:\n                # Log failure but don't block response\n                print(f\"Warning: Failed to log to tool_logs: {e}\")\n", "line_end": 2183, "line_start": 2164}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/response.py", "byte_size": 98520, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 12, "line_count": 2367, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/response.py", "sha256": "1028c42f8e1a2ec28b1221a03203296a794018eac473de95d15df2f1cb9d95f4"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:56:46.154599Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:56:46 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 16689, "byte_start": 15862, "content": "async def append_line(\n    path: Path,\n    line: str,\n    use_wal: bool = True,\n    repo_root: Optional[Path] = None,\n    context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Bulletproof append a single line to the provided file path.\n\n    Args:\n        path: File path to append to\n        line: Line content to append\n        use_wal: Whether to use Write-Ahead Log for crash safety\n    \"\"\"\n    path = _ensure_safe_path(\n        path,\n        operation=\"append\",\n        context=context or {\"component\": \"logs\"},\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n    )\n    await ensure_parent(path, repo_root=repo_root, context=context)\n\n    if use_wal:\n        await asyncio.to_thread(_write_line_with_wal, path, line, repo_root, context)\n    else:\n        await asyncio.to_thread(_write_line, path, line, True, repo_root, context)\n\n", "line_end": 543, "line_start": 516}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/files.py", "byte_size": 24257, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 4, "line_count": 784, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/files.py", "sha256": "ec11343c25d1f3f37b69d201a920c7e09a3eff0fe3a28f515d0b00d3f5481d1c"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T05:56:46.369979Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 05:56:46 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 28054, "byte_start": 26794, "content": "                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scribe_sessions (\n                        session_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n                        transport_session_id TEXT,\n                        agent_id TEXT,\n                        repo_root TEXT,\n                        mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (mode IN ('sentinel','project')) DEFAULT 'sentinel',\n                        started_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        last_active_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scribe_sessions_transport ON scribe_sessions(transport_session_id);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_scribe_sessions_agent ON scribe_sessions(agent_id);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session_projects (\n                        session_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n                        project_name TEXT,\n                        updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        FOREIGN KEY(project_name) REFERENCES scribe_projects(name) ON DELETE SET NULL\n                    );\n", "line_end": 768, "line_start": 746}, "frontmatter": {}, [ℹ️] [2026-01-04 06:05:02 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 7200, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "# Architecture: Tool Logging Recursion Fix\n\n**Status:** Ready for Implementation\n**Created:** 2026-01-04\n**Author:** ArchitectAgent\n**Project:** scribe_tool_output_refinement\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis document specifies the architecture for eliminating the recursive `append_entry` pattern in `finalize_tool_response()` by implementing a direct JSONL+SQL dual-write system for tool call logging.\n\n**Key Design Principles:**\n- **Zero Recursion:** Direct file/SQL writes bypass `append_entry` entirely\n- **Session-Aware:** All tool calls linked to stable `session_id` from `scribe_sessions` table\n- **Crash-Safe:** Reuses existing WAL-based atomic write infrastructure\n- **No Reminders:** Tool logs are audit trails, explicitly excluded from reminder system\n- **Backward Compatible:** Maintains existing JSONL format for tool logs\n\n---\n\n## Problem Statement\n\n### Current State (BROKEN - VERIFIED AT CODE LEVEL)\n\n**File:** `utils/response.py:2164-2183`\n\n```python\n# Current implementation - RECURSIVE PATTERN\nif tool_name != \"append_entry\":\n    await append_entry(\n        message=f\"Tool call: {tool_name}\",\n        log_type=\"tool_logs\",\n        meta={...},\n        format=\"structured\"\n    )\n```\n\n**Issues:**\n1. **Recursion:** `finalize_tool_response()` \u2192 `append_entry()` \u2192 `finalize_tool_response()` \u2192 infinite loop\n2. **Guard Insufficient:** Only prevents `append_entry` from logging itself, all other tools still recurse\n3. **Token Bloat:** Creates massive JSON nesting in tool responses\n4. **Performance:** Unnecessary overhead for every tool call\n5. **No SQL Metrics:** Tool usage data only in per-project JSONL files, no cross-project analytics\n\n### Root Cause Analysis\n\nThe recursion occurs because:\n- `append_entry` is itself a tool that calls `finalize_tool_response()`\n- `finalize_tool_response()` tries to log ALL tool calls via `append_entry(log_type=\"tool_logs\")`\n- Guard only prevents one level, doesn't stop other tools from recursing\n\n---\n\n## Solution Overview\n\n### NEW Architecture: Direct JSONL+SQL Dual-Write\n\n```\nTool Call \u2192 finalize_tool_response() \u2192 log_tool_call()\n                                            \u251c\u2500\u2192 Direct JSONL write (utils/files.py::append_line)\n                                            \u2514\u2500\u2192 Direct SQL insert (storage/sqlite.py::record_tool_call)\n\nNO CALLS TO append_entry() - params.append(end_time)\n\n    where_sql = \" AND \".join(where_clauses)\n\n    async with self._conn_lock:\n        cursor = await self._conn.execute(\n            f\"\"\"\n            SELECT\n        \"\"\",\n            tuple(params)\n        )\n        row = await cursor.fetchone()\n        return dict(row) if row else {}\n```\n\n---\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Tool Call Lifecycle\n\n```\n1. DocumentChangeRecord, SyncStatusRecord\n)\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.time import format_utc, utcnow\nfrom scribe_mcp.utils.search import message_matches\n\nSQLITE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass SQLiteStorage(StorageBackend):\n    \"\"\"SQLite-backed persistence with lazy connections.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, db_path: Path   str) -> None:\n        self._path = Path(db_path).expanduser()\n        self._init_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._write_lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._initialised = False\n\n    async def setup(self) -> None:\n        await self._initialise()\n\n    async def close(self) -> None:  # pragma: no cover - nothing persistent to close\n        # Connections are short-lived; nothing to do.\n        return None\n\n async with self._write_lock:\n            await self._execute(\n            \"\"\"\n            INSERT INTO scribe_projects (name, repo_root, progress_log_path)\n            VALUES (?, ?, ?)\n            ON CONFLICT(name)\n            DO UPDATE SET repo_root = excluded.repo_root,\n                          progress_log_path = excluded.progress_log_path;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name, repo_root, progress_log_path),\n        )\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            WHERE name = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name,),\n        )\n        return ProjectRecord(\n            id=row[\"id\"],\n            name=row[\"name\"],\n            repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n            progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n        )\n\n    async def fetch_project(self, name: str) -> Optional[ProjectRecord]:\n        await self._initialise()\n        row = await self._fetchone(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            WHERE name = ?;\n            \"\"\",\n            (name,),\n        )\n        if not row:\n            return None\n        return ProjectRecord(\n            id=row[\"id\"],\n            name=row[\"name\"],\n            repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n            progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n        )\n\n    async def list_projects(self) -> List[ProjectRecord]:\n        await self._initialise()\n        rows = await self._fetchall(\n            \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, name, repo_root, progress_log_path\n            FROM scribe_projects\n            ORDER BY name;\n            \"\"\"\n        )\n        records: List[ProjectRecord] = []\n        for row in rows:\n            records.append(\n                ProjectRecord(\n                    id=row[\"id\"],\n                    name=row[\"name\"],\n                    repo_root=row[\"repo_root\"],\n                    progress_log_path=row[\"progress_log_path\"],\n                )\n            )\n        return records\n\n    async def delete_project(self, name: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Delete a project and all associated data with proper cascade handling.\"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n\n        # First check if project exists\n        project = await self.fetch_project(name)\n        if not project:\n            return False\n\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            # Delete project and all related data using proper cascade order\n            # SQLite foreign key constraints should handle most of this automatically,\n            # but we'll be explicit for safety and clarity\n\n scribe_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS [ℹ️] [2026-01-04 06:06:00 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 47369, "byte_start": 42819, "content": "                        VALUES (new.id, new.document_type, new.section_id, new.content);\n                    END\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_sections_fts_delete\n                    AFTER DELETE ON document_sections BEGIN\n                        INSERT INTO document_sections_fts(document_sections_fts, rowid, document_type, section_id, content)\n                        VALUES ('delete', old.id, old.document_type, old.section_id, old.content);\n                    END\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS document_sections_fts_update\n                    AFTER UPDATE ON document_sections BEGIN\n                        INSERT INTO document_sections_fts(document_sections_fts, rowid, document_type, section_id, content)\n                        VALUES ('delete', old.id, old.document_type, old.section_id, old.content);\n                        INSERT INTO document_sections_fts(rowid, document_type, section_id, content)\n                        VALUES (new.id, new.document_type, new.section_id, new.content);\n                    END\n                    \"\"\",\n                ]\n            )\n            # Ensure scribe_entries has metadata columns for categorization and prioritization\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_entries\", \"priority\", \"TEXT DEFAULT 'medium'\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_entries\", \"category\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_entries\", \"tags\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"scribe_entries\", \"confidence\", \"REAL DEFAULT 1.0\")\n            await self._migrate_document_sections()\n            await self._ensure_column(\"document_changes\", \"project_root\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"document_changes\", \"file_path\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"sync_status\", \"project_root\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"sync_status\", \"relative_path\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"document_sections\", \"project_root\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"document_sections\", \"file_path\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_column(\"document_sections\", \"relative_path\", \"TEXT\")\n            await self._ensure_index(\"CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_sections_file_path ON document_sections(project_root, file_path);\")\n            await self._ensure_index(\"CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sync_status_file_path ON sync_status(project_root, file_path);\")\n            await self._ensure_index(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_report_cards_project_agent ON agent_report_cards(project_id, agent_name);\")\n            await self._ensure_index(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_agent_report_cards_stage ON agent_report_cards(stage);\")\n            # Tool Calls Tracking Table (for direct logging without recursion)\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tool_calls (\n                        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n                        session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        tool_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                        timestamp TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n                        duration_ms REAL,\n                        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'success' CHECK (status IN ('success', 'error', 'partial')),\n                        format_requested TEXT,\n                        project_name TEXT,\n                        agent_id TEXT,\n                        error_message TEXT,\n                        response_size_bytes INTEGER,\n                        FOREIGN KEY (session_id) REFERENCES scribe_sessions(session_id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n                    );\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_session ON tool_calls(session_id);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_tool_name ON tool_calls(tool_name);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_timestamp ON tool_calls(timestamp);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    \"\"\"\n                    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tool_calls_project ON tool_calls(project_name);\n                    \"\"\",\n                    # Document Management 2.0 Indexes\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_sections_project ON document_sections(project_id);\",\n                    \"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_document_sections_updated ON document_sections(updated_at);\",\n", "line_end": 1035, "line_start": 1004}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py", "byte_size": 90933, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 11, "line_count": 2145, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "storage/sqlite.py", "sha256": "86fbe32e609548bf7991001b59a8dfe0d2faf2a143662cba9a617e9bdceb6493"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T06:11:08.108853Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 06:11:13 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 87076, "byte_start": 84988, "content": "    async def record_tool_call(\n        self,\n        session_id: str,\n        tool_name: str,\n        duration_ms: Optional[float] = None,\n        status: str = \"success\",\n        format_requested: Optional[str] = None,\n        project_name: Optional[str] = None,\n        agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n        error_message: Optional[str] = None,\n        response_size_bytes: Optional[int] = None\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record a tool call to the database for analytics.\n\n        This method provides direct SQL logging without triggering append_entry,\n        preventing recursion in finalize_tool_response().\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session identifier from scribe_sessions table\n            tool_name: Name of the tool that was called\n            duration_ms: Optional execution time in milliseconds\n            status: Tool execution status (success, error, partial)\n            format_requested: Format parameter from tool call (readable/structured/compact)\n            project_name: Optional project context\n            agent_id: Optional agent identifier\n            error_message: Optional error details if status=error\n            response_size_bytes: Optional response payload size for cost tracking\n        \"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            try:\n                await self._execute(\n                    \"\"\"\n                    INSERT INTO tool_calls (\n                        session_id, tool_name, timestamp, duration_ms, status,\n                        format_requested, project_name, agent_id, error_message, response_size_bytes\n                    ) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'), ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                    \"\"\",\n                    (session_id, tool_name, duration_ms, status,\n                     format_requested, project_name, agent_id, error_message, response_size_bytes)\n                )\n            except Exception as e:\n                # Log error but don't raise - tool logging should never block tool execution\n                logger.error(f\"Failed to record tool call: {e}\")\n", "line_end": 2030, "line_start": 1987}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, [ℹ️] [2026-01-04 06:11:17 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 88107, "byte_start": 87077, "content": "    async def get_session_tool_calls(\n        self,\n        session_id: str,\n        limit: Optional[int] = None\n    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n        \"\"\"Get tool call history for a session.\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session identifier\n            limit: Optional maximum number of results\n\n        Returns:\n            List of tool call records ordered by timestamp (newest first)\n        \"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n\n        query = \"\"\"\n            SELECT id, tool_name, timestamp, duration_ms, status,\n                   format_requested, project_name, agent_id, error_message, response_size_bytes\n            FROM tool_calls\n            WHERE session_id = ?\n            ORDER BY timestamp DESC\n        \"\"\"\n\n        if limit:\n            query += f\" LIMIT {int(limit)}\"\n\n        rows = await self._fetchall(query, (session_id,))\n        return [dict(row) for row in rows]\n\n    async def get_tool_metrics(\n        self,\n        tool_name: Optional[str] = None,\n        project_name: Optional[str] = None,\n", "line_end": 2065, "line_start": 2032}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/storage/sqlite.py", "byte_size": 90933, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 11, "line_count": 2145, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "storage/sqlite.py", "sha256": "86fbe32e609548bf7991001b59a8dfe0d2faf2a143662cba9a617e9bdceb6493"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T06:11:17.924025Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 06:13:26 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 740, "byte_start": 0, "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Test script to verify tool_calls table schema and methods.\"\"\"\n\nimport asyncio\nimport sqlite3\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n\n# Add parent directory to path for imports\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))\n\nfrom scribe_mcp.storage.sqlite import SQLiteStorage\n\n\nasync def test_schema_creation():\n    \"\"\"Test that tool_calls table and indexes are created.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"TEST 1: Schema Creation\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Create temporary database\n    test_db = Path(\"/tmp/test_tool_calls.db\")\n    if test_db.exists():\n        test_db.unlink()\n\n    storage = SQLiteStorage(test_db)\n    await storage.setup()\n\n    # Check table exists\n", "line_end": 30, "line_start": 1}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/tests/test_tool_calls_schema.py", "byte_size": 8741, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 2, "line_count": 282, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "tests/test_tool_calls_schema.py", "sha256": "9dcc7586978458bc96ec17f243e5a0e42cfcab97d2260f9f4e1bf68b0ad0c15b"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T06:13:26.938153Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

Args:\\n            session_id: Session identifier from scribe_sessions table\\n            tool_name: Name of the tool that was called\\n            duration_ms: Optional execution time in [ℹ️] [2026-01-04 10:35:10 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 17764, "byte_start": 15862, "content": "async def append_line(\n    path: Path,\n    line: str,\n    use_wal: bool = True,\n    repo_root: Optional[Path] = None,\n    context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Bulletproof append a single line to the provided file path.\n\n    Args:\n        path: File path to append to\n        line: Line content to append\n        use_wal: Whether to use Write-Ahead Log for crash safety\n    \"\"\"\n    path = _ensure_safe_path(\n        path,\n        operation=\"append\",\n        context=context or {\"component\": \"logs\"},\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n    )\n    await ensure_parent(path, repo_root=repo_root, context=context)\n\n    if use_wal:\n        await asyncio.to_thread(_write_line_with_wal, path, line, repo_root, context)\n    else:\n        await asyncio.to_thread(_write_line, path, line, True, repo_root, context)\n\n\ndef _write_line_with_wal(\n    path: Path,\n    line: str,\n    repo_root: Optional[Path] = None,\n    context: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Write line with Write-Ahead Log for crash safety.\"\"\"\n    path = _ensure_safe_path(\n        path,\n        operation=\"append\",\n        context=context or {\"component\": \"wal\"},\n        repo_root=repo_root,\n    )\n    wal = WriteAheadLog(path, repo_root=repo_root, context=context)\n\n    # Journal the operation first\n    entry_id = wal.write_entry({\n        'op': 'append',\n        'content': line if line.endswith('\\n') else line + '\\n',\n        'file_path': str(path)\n    })\n\n    try:\n        # Apply the operation with file locking\n        with file_lock(path, 'a', repo_root=repo_root) as f:\n            f.write(line)\n            if not line.endswith(\"\\n\"):\n                f.write(\"\\n\")\n            f.flush()\n            os.fsync(f.fileno())\n\n        # Commit the operation\n        wal.commit_entry(entry_id)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Warning: Failed to append line {entry_id}: {e}\")\n        raise\n", "line_end": 580, "line_start": 516}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/files.py", "byte_size": 24257, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 4, "line_count": 784, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/files.py", "sha256": "ec11343c25d1f3f37b69d201a920c7e09a3eff0fe3a28f515d0b00d3f5481d1c"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T10:35:10.807348Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

84988, "content": "    async def record_tool_call(\n        self,\n        session_id: str,\n        tool_name: str,\n        duration_ms: Optional[float] = None,\n        status: str = \"success\",\n        format_requested: Optional[str] = None,\n        project_name: Optional[str] = None,\n        agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n        error_message: Optional[str] = None,\n        response_size_bytes: Optional[int] = None\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record a tool call to the database for analytics.\n\n        This method provides direct SQL logging without triggering append_entry,\n        preventing recursion in finalize_tool_response().\n\n        Args:\n            session_id: Session identifier from scribe_sessions table\n            tool_name: Name of the tool that was called\n            duration_ms: Optional execution time in milliseconds\n            status: Tool execution status (success, error, partial)\n            format_requested: Format parameter from tool call (readable/structured/compact)\n            project_name: Optional project context\n            agent_id: Optional agent identifier\n            error_message: Optional error details if status=error\n            response_size_bytes: Optional response payload size for cost tracking\n        \"\"\"\n        await self._initialise()\n        async with self._write_lock:\n            try:\n                await self._execute(\n                    \"\"\"\n                    INSERT INTO tool_calls (\n                        session_id, tool_name, timestamp, duration_ms, status,\n                        format_requested, project_name, agent_id, error_message, response_size_bytes\n                    ) VALUES (?, ?, datetime('now'), ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n                    \"\"\",\n                    (session_id, tool_name, duration_ms, status,\n                     format_requested, project_name, agent_id, error_message, response_size_bytes)\n                )\n            except Exception as e:\n                # Log error but don't raise - tool logging should never block tool execution\n                logger.error(f\"Failed to record tool call: {e}\")\n", [ℹ️] [2026-01-04 10:35:11 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 7685, "byte_start": 5973, "content": "            def derive_session_identity(exec_context, arguments: dict) -> tuple[str, dict]:\n                \"\"\"Derive stable session identity from execution context.\n\n                Returns (identity_hash, identity_parts dict)\n                \"\"\"\n                # 1. Canonicalize repo_root\n                repo_root = os.path.realpath(exec_context.repo_root)\n\n                # 2. Get mode and scope_key\n                mode = exec_context.mode  # \"project\" or \"sentinel\"\n                if mode == \"sentinel\":\n                    scope_key = exec_context.sentinel_day  # e.g., \"2026-01-03\"\n                else:\n                    scope_key = exec_context.execution_id  # UUID\n\n                # 3. Get agent_key (prefer stable ID, fallback to display_name)\n                agent_key = None\n                if exec_context.agent_identity:\n                    agent_key = (\n                        getattr(exec_context.agent_identity, 'id', None) or\n                        getattr(exec_context.agent_identity, 'instance_id', None) or\n                        exec_context.agent_identity.display_name\n                    )\n                if not agent_key:\n                    agent_key = arguments.get(\"agent\") or \"default\"\n\n                # 4. Construct identity string\n                identity = f\"{repo_root}:{mode}:{scope_key}:{agent_key}\"\n\n                # 5. Hash it (full SHA-256, no truncation)\n                identity_hash = hashlib.sha256(identity.encode()).hexdigest()\n\n                return identity_hash, {\n                    \"repo_root\": repo_root,\n                    \"mode\": mode,\n                    \"scope_key\": scope_key,\n                    \"agent_key\": agent_key,\n                }\n\n", "line_end": 200, "line_start": 162}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/server.py", "byte_size": 26996, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 4, "line_count": 619, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "server.py", "sha256": "b5ea3b09b035866838266679c960fcec0dfe4ed1e244e55d768ffe002a7b5a92"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T10:35:11.244157Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

let it fail loudly\n                            state = await state_manager.load()\n                            session_mode = state.get_session_mode(context_payload.get(\"session_id\"))\n                    # Default to sentinel to avoid implicit project scope or audit pollution.\n                    context_payload[\"mode\"] = session_mode or \"sentinel\"\n", "line_end": 330, "line_start": 270}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/server.py", "byte_size": 26996, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 4, "line_count": 619, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "server.py", "sha256": "b5ea3b09b035866838266679c960fcec0dfe4ed1e244e55d768ffe002a7b5a92"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T10:35:15.843976Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 10:35:35 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 4349, "byte_start": 1254, "content": "class RouterContextManager:\n    \"\"\"Owns router-generated session/execution identity and current context.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, storage_backend=None) -> None:\n        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n        self._transport_sessions: Dict[str, str] = {}  # Keep as performance cache\n        self._process_instance_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        self._storage_backend = storage_backend  # NEW: Injected dependency\n\n    async def get_or_create_session_id(self, transport_session_id: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"\n        Get or create a stable session ID for the given transport session ID.\n\n        Lookup order:\n        1. In-memory cache (fast path)\n        2. Database lookup (persistence layer)\n        3. Create new session and persist\n\n        Args:\n            transport_session_id: Unstable ID from transport layer\n\n        Returns:\n            Stable session UUID that persists across restarts\n        \"\"\"\n        if not transport_session_id:\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext requires transport_session_id\")\n\n        async with self._lock:\n            # TIER 1: Check in-memory cache (fast path)\n            existing = self._transport_sessions.get(transport_session_id)\n            if existing:\n                return existing\n\n            # TIER 2: Check database for existing session (persistence layer)\n            if self._storage_backend and hasattr(self._storage_backend, \"get_session_by_transport\"):\n                # NO SILENT ERRORS - let it fail loudly so we can see what's broken\n                db_session = await self._storage_backend.get_session_by_transport(transport_session_id)\n                if db_session and db_session.get(\"session_id\"):\n                    session_id = db_session[\"session_id\"]\n                    # Cache it for future requests (performance optimization)\n                    self._transport_sessions[transport_session_id] = session_id\n                    return session_id\n\n            # TIER 3: Create new session (not found in cache or DB)\n            session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n\n            # Cache immediately\n            self._transport_sessions[transport_session_id] = session_id\n\n            # TIER 3b: Persist to database immediately\n            if self._storage_backend and hasattr(self._storage_backend, \"upsert_session\"):\n                # NO SILENT ERRORS - let it fail loudly so we can see what's broken\n                await self._storage_backend.upsert_session(\n                    session_id=session_id,\n                    transport_session_id=transport_session_id,\n                    repo_root=None,  # Will be set later by set_project\n                    mode=\"sentinel\",  # Default mode\n                )\n\n            return session_id\n\n    def _build_agent_identity(self, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> AgentIdentity:\n        agent_kind = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_AGENT_KIND\", \"other\")\n        model = os.environ.get(\"SCRIBE_AGENT_MODEL\") or os.environ.get(\"CODEX_MODEL\")\n        sub_id = None\n        display_name = None\n        raw_identity = payload.get(\"agent_identity\")\n        if isinstance(raw_identity, dict):\n", "line_end": 120, "line_start": 53}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/shared/execution_context.py", "byte_size": 7745, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 194, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "shared/execution_context.py", "sha256": "12d7aee9a33a1ffc5cf0c1572e392d74389e6d42fd76eb920759667b01f8f465"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T10:35:35.537614Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log

[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 10:38:59 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 7745, "byte_start": 5501, "content": "        if not isinstance(affected, list):\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext affected_dev_projects must be a list\")\n\n        session_id = payload.get(\"session_id\")\n        if session_id is not None and not isinstance(session_id, str):\n            raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext session_id must be a string\")\n        if not session_id:\n            transport_session_id = payload.get(\"transport_session_id\")\n            if transport_session_id is not None and not isinstance(transport_session_id, str):\n                raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext transport_session_id must be a string\")\n            if not transport_session_id:\n                raise ValueError(\"ExecutionContext requires transport_session_id or session_id\")\n            session_id = await self.get_or_create_session_id(transport_session_id)\n        else:\n            transport_session_id = payload.get(\"transport_session_id\")\n        execution_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        timestamp_utc = _utc_now_iso()\n\n        sentinel_day = None\n        if mode == \"sentinel\":\n            sentinel_day = timestamp_utc.split(\"T\", 1)[0]\n\n        agent_identity = self._build_agent_identity(payload)\n\n        return ExecutionContext(\n            repo_root=repo_root,\n            mode=mode,\n            session_id=session_id,\n            execution_id=execution_id,\n            agent_identity=agent_identity,\n            intent=intent,\n            timestamp_utc=timestamp_utc,\n            affected_dev_projects=[str(item) for item in affected],\n            sentinel_day=sentinel_day,\n            transport_session_id=transport_session_id,\n            stable_session_id=payload.get(\"stable_session_id\"),  # NEW - pass through stable session\n            bug_id=payload.get(\"bug_id\"),\n            security_id=payload.get(\"security_id\"),\n            parent_execution_id=payload.get(\"parent_execution_id\"),\n            toolchain=payload.get(\"toolchain\"),\n        )\n\n    def set_current(self, context: ExecutionContext) -> contextvars.Token:\n        return _CURRENT_CONTEXT.set(context)\n\n    def reset(self, token: contextvars.Token) -> None:\n        _CURRENT_CONTEXT.reset(token)\n\n    def get_current(self) -> Optional[ExecutionContext]:\n        return _CURRENT_CONTEXT.get()\n", "line_end": 194, "line_start": 145}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/shared/execution_context.py", "byte_size": 7745, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 194, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "shared/execution_context.py", "sha256": "12d7aee9a33a1ffc5cf0c1572e392d74389e6d42fd76eb920759667b01f8f465"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T10:38:59.265381Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
