[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 11:45:18 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunk": {"byte_end": 93055, "byte_start": 87917, "content": "    async def finalize_tool_response(\n        self,\n        data: Dict[str, Any],\n        format: str = \"readable\",  # NOTE: readable is DEFAULT\n        tool_name: str = \"\"\n    ) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], \"CallToolResult\"]:\n        \"\"\"\n        CRITICAL ROUTER: Logs tool call to tool_logs, then formats response.\n\n        This method ensures complete audit trail by logging structured JSON\n        BEFORE formatting for display.\n\n        ISSUE #9962 FIX: When format=\"readable\", we return CallToolResult with\n        TextContent ONLY (no structuredContent). This forces Claude Code to\n        display the text cleanly with actual newlines instead of escaped \\\\n.\n\n        Args:\n            data: Tool response data (always a dict)\n            format: Output format - \"readable\", \"structured\", \"compact\", or \"both\"\n            tool_name: Name of the tool being called\n\n        Returns:\n            - format=\"readable\": CallToolResult with TextContent only (clean display)\n            - format=\"both\": CallToolResult with TextContent + structuredContent\n            - format=\"structured\"/\"compact\": Original data dict\n            - Fallback to dict if MCP types unavailable\n        \"\"\"\n        # STEP 1: Log to tool_logs (audit trail) - SKIP for append_entry to avoid recursion\n        # append_entry calls finalize_tool_response, which would call append_entry again\n        if tool_name != \"append_entry\":\n            try:\n                from tools.append_entry import append_entry\n\n                await append_entry(\n                    message=f\"Tool call: {tool_name}\",\n                    log_type=\"tool_logs\",\n                    meta={\n                        \"tool\": tool_name,\n                        \"format_requested\": format,\n                        \"response_data\": data,\n                        \"timestamp\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + \"Z\"\n                    },\n                    format=\"structured\"  # Use structured to avoid nested formatting\n                )\n            except Exception as e:\n                # Log failure but don't block response\n                print(f\"Warning: Failed to log to tool_logs: {e}\")\n\n        # STEP 2: Format based on parameter\n        if format == self.FORMAT_READABLE:\n            # PRIORITY 1: Check if integration code already populated readable_content\n            # (Used by list_projects, get_project, set_project with new formatters)\n            if 'readable_content' in data:\n                readable_content = data['readable_content']\n            # PRIORITY 2: Check for errors\n            elif data.get('ok') == False or 'error' in data:\n                readable_content = self.format_readable_error(\n                    data.get('error', 'Unknown error'),\n                    data\n                )\n            # PRIORITY 3: Route to appropriate readable formatter based on tool\n            elif tool_name == \"read_file\":\n                readable_content = self.format_readable_file_content(data)\n            elif tool_name in [\"read_recent\", \"query_entries\"]:\n                # Pass search context for query_entries to show filters\n                search_context = None\n                if tool_name == \"query_entries\":\n                    # Extract search parameters from data (prefixed with search_)\n                    search_context = {}\n                    if 'search_message' in data:\n                        search_context['message'] = data['search_message']\n                    if 'search_status' in data:\n                        search_context['status'] = data['search_status']\n                    if 'search_agents' in data:\n                        search_context['agents'] = data['search_agents']\n                    if 'search_emoji' in data:\n                        search_context['emoji'] = data['search_emoji']\n                    # Always show search header for query_entries even if no filters\n                    if not search_context:\n                        search_context = {'_is_search': True}\n\n                readable_content = self.format_readable_log_entries(\n                    data.get('entries', []),\n                    data.get('pagination', {}),\n                    search_context=search_context if search_context else None,\n                    project_name=data.get('project_name')\n                )\n            elif tool_name == \"append_entry\":\n                readable_content = self.format_readable_append_entry(data)\n            else:\n                # Generic readable format for unknown tools\n                readable_content = json.dumps(data, indent=2)\n\n            # ISSUE #9962 FIX: Return CallToolResult with TextContent ONLY\n            # This forces Claude Code to display text cleanly (no escaped \\n)\n            if MCP_TYPES_AVAILABLE and CallToolResult and TextContent:\n                return CallToolResult(\n                    content=[TextContent(type=\"text\", text=readable_content)]\n                    # NO structuredContent = Claude Code renders text cleanly!\n                )\n            else:\n                # Fallback for environments without MCP SDK\n                return {\n                    \"ok\": True,\n", "line_end": 2240, "line_start": 2137}, "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "line_range", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/response.py", "byte_size": 98520, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 12, "line_count": 2367, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/response.py", "sha256": "1028c42f8e1a2ec28b1221a03203296a794018eac473de95d15df2f1cb9d95f4"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T11:45:18.947094Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
[ℹ️] [2026-01-04 11:45:19 UTC] [Agent: Scribe] [Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement] Tool call: read_file | format_requested=readable; response_data={"chunks": [{"byte_end": 5985, "byte_start": 0, "chunk_index": 0, "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nTool logging module with zero recursion guarantee.\n\nCRITICAL DESIGN CONSTRAINTS:\n- ZERO imports from tools/append_entry or utils/response\n- ZERO imports from any module that transitively imports the above\n- Only imports: utils/files, storage/, config/, standard library\n- Direct writes to JSONL + SQL, completely bypassing append_entry\n\nThis module breaks the recursion cycle:\n  finalize_tool_response() -> append_entry(log_type=\"tool_logs\") -> finalize_tool_response() -> ...\n\nBy providing direct JSONL + SQL logging without any tool invocation.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Dict, Optional\n\n# Safe imports - these do NOT import append_entry or response\n# CRITICAL: Import settings only (no other utils imports to avoid response.py)\nfrom scribe_mcp.config.settings import settings\n\n\n# Minimal JSONL append function (inlined to avoid importing utils.files)\ndef _append_jsonl_line(path: Path, line: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Minimal JSONL line append without dependencies.\n\n    This is inlined to avoid importing utils.files which would\n    trigger utils/__init__.py importing response.py.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Ensure parent directory exists\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Append line with newline\n    with open(path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        f.write(line)\n        if not line.endswith('\\n'):\n            f.write('\\n')\n        f.flush()\n        os.fsync(f.fileno())\n\n\ndef get_tool_log_path() -> Path:\n    \"\"\"\n    Get path to TOOL_LOG.jsonl file.\n\n    Returns:\n        Path to .scribe/logs/TOOL_LOG.jsonl\n    \"\"\"\n    return settings.project_root / \".scribe\" / \"logs\" / \"TOOL_LOG.jsonl\"\n\n\n# NOTE: extract_session_context() removed to avoid circular imports.\n# The entire scribe_mcp module hierarchy imports utils.response transitively.\n# Solution: Callers must pass session_id explicitly to log_tool_call().\n\n\ndef log_tool_call(\n    tool_name: str,\n    session_id: str,\n    duration_ms: Optional[float] = None,\n    status: str = \"success\",\n    format_requested: Optional[str] = None,\n    project_name: Optional[str] = None,\n    agent_id: Optional[str] = None,\n    error_message: Optional[str] = None,\n    response_size_bytes: Optional[int] = None,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Log tool call to JSONL without recursion.\n\n    CRITICAL: Never calls append_entry or finalize_tool_response.\n    CRITICAL: Synchronous function to avoid async complexity.\n\n    Write strategy:\n    1. JSONL: Append to .scribe/logs/TOOL_LOG.jsonl via minimal inline function\n    2. SQL: Handled separately by finalize_tool_response after logging completes\n\n    Graceful degradation: Log errors but never raise (tool logging can't break tools).\n\n    Args:\n        tool_name: Name of the tool that was called\n        session_id: Session identifier from execution context\n        duration_ms: Optional execution time in milliseconds\n        status: Tool execution status (success, error, partial)\n        format_requested: Format parameter from tool call (readable/structured/compact)\n        project_name: Optional project context\n        agent_id: Optional agent identifier\n        error_message: Optional error details if status=error\n        response_size_bytes: Optional response payload size for cost tracking\n\n    Returns:\n        None - all errors are caught and logged to stderr\n    \"\"\"\n    # Build JSONL entry\n    timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n\n    entry = {\n        \"timestamp\": timestamp,\n        \"session_id\": session_id,\n        \"tool_name\": tool_name,\n        \"status\": status,\n    }\n\n    # Add optional fields only if present\n    if duration_ms is not None:\n        entry[\"duration_ms\"] = duration_ms\n    if format_requested:\n        entry[\"format_requested\"] = format_requested\n    if project_name:\n        entry[\"project_name\"] = project_name\n    if agent_id:\n        entry[\"agent_id\"] = agent_id\n    if error_message:\n        entry[\"error_message\"] = error_message\n    if response_size_bytes is not None:\n        entry[\"response_size_bytes\"] = response_size_bytes\n\n    # Write to JSONL (always attempt this)\n    try:\n        jsonl_path = get_tool_log_path()\n        json_line = json.dumps(entry, ensure_ascii=False)\n        _append_jsonl_line(jsonl_path, json_line)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        # Log to stderr but don't raise - JSONL write failure shouldn't break tools\n        print(f\"Warning: Failed to write tool log to JSONL: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n\n\n# Module-level verification: ensure no imports of recursion sources\ndef _verify_no_recursion_imports():\n    \"\"\"\n    Static verification that this module doesn't import recursion sources.\n\n    This function is for documentation/testing purposes only.\n    Run at module load time in debug mode to catch import violations early.\n\n    NOTE: Must be run via direct file execution, not via python -m\n    because -m loads the entire scribe_mcp package hierarchy which\n    transitively imports response.py.\n    \"\"\"\n    import sys\n    forbidden = {\n        \"scribe_mcp.tools.append_entry\",\n        \"scribe_mcp.utils.response\",\n        \"tools.append_entry\",\n        \"utils.response\",\n    }\n\n    current_modules = set(sys.modules.keys())\n    violations = current_modules & forbidden\n\n    if violations:\n        raise ImportError(\n            f\"CRITICAL: tool_logger.py has imported forbidden modules: {violations}\\n\"\n            f\"This will cause infinite recursion. Remove these imports immediately.\"\n        )\n\n\n# Run verification in debug mode\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Add parent directory to path for direct imports\n    import sys\n    from pathlib import Path\n    sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent))\n\n    # Now import and verify\n    _verify_no_recursion_imports()\n    print(\"\u2705 tool_logger.py: No recursion imports detected\")\n    print(f\"\u2705 Verified clean imports: only config.settings\")\n    print(f\"\u2705 log_tool_call() ready for use\")\n", "line_end": 177, "line_start": 1}], "frontmatter": {}, "frontmatter_byte_count": 0, "frontmatter_line_count": 0, "frontmatter_raw": "", "has_frontmatter": false, "mode": "chunk", "ok": true, "reminders": [{"category": "context", "context": "Entries:    Last: ", "emoji": "\ud83c\udfaf", "level": "info", "message": "Project: scribe_tool_output_refinement", "score": 3, "tone": "neutral"}], "scan": {"absolute_path": "/home/austin/projects/MCP_SPINE/scribe_mcp/utils/tool_logger.py", "byte_size": 5985, "encoding": "utf-8", "estimated_chunk_count": 1, "line_count": 177, "newline_type": "LF", "repo_relative_path": "utils/tool_logger.py", "sha256": "fe96fdf1a33516969190f771dd877199c0379fe3b1eba0d616c1924f40e977ef"}}; timestamp=2026-01-04T11:45:19.155508Z; tool=read_file; priority=medium; log_type=tool_logs; content_type=log
